Task ID: 19 (M7.0.0 Phase 7 — Open Terminal Here)
Agent: main (Super Z)
Task: From the Files explorer, open a NORMAL Alpine Linux terminal session whose guest working directory is the exact directory currently browsed — Linux area only, honest boundary for Android areas, existing sessions untouched. Single isolated commit; P8/P9 explicitly out of scope.

Work Log:
- Architecture re-verified against source before any code: openLinuxShell (preflight → prepareLinuxSession on IO → spawnLinuxSession on Main → onReady) is the canonical normal-session path; command apps deliver the chain as PTY ARGV (`sh -l -c "<chain>"`, the m3.5 lesson — never a PTY write); guestLaunchChain's quoting is a registry-token ALLOWLIST regex (safe for plain command names, NOT for arbitrary paths — PathSafety legitimately accepts spaces, apostrophes, quotes, $, ;, &&, |, backticks, newlines); FilesViewModel.state.path already holds the explorer's current directory as the ONE validated AreaPath; only FilesViewModel implements FilesOpsSurface.
- NEW files/TerminalLaunch.kt (pure, JVM-tested): TerminalLaunch(areaId, kind, directory: AreaPath) — carries the SAME AreaPath instance the explorer state holds (no re-validation, no second path representation; init{} refuses non-LINUX kinds at construction); TerminalLaunchResolution sealed = Ready(launch) | NotSupported(reason) — no third state; openTerminalHereProblem(kind): GUEST_LINUX → null, both Android kinds → TerminalLaunchSupport.ANDROID_BOUNDARY_MESSAGE (the exact product sentence, one constant, pinned).
- apps/CommandApps.kt: SIBLING helper guestTerminalChain(directory, guestShell) = `cd -- '<dir>' && exec <guestShell> -l` — POSIX single-quote wrapping ('→'\''), `--` ends option parsing (dash-leading paths stay paths), `&&` (never `;`) so the interactive login shell only follows a SUCCESSFUL cd (a vanished directory exits honestly instead of silently landing in $HOME), trailing exec lands a real interactive prompt. guestLaunchChain untouched. The directory remains DATA end to end.
- TerminalViewModel.openLinuxShellAt(directory, onReady): mirrors openLinuxShell line for line — same RuntimeProcessLauncher preflight, prepareLinuxSession on IO, spawnLinuxSession on Main, honest launchError, onReady only after real spawn-and-select — with the command-app argv shape carrying guestTerminalChain; session titled "Alpine Linux" (a NORMAL session). No new PTY path, no session reuse, no writes into running PTYs.
- FilesViewModel.terminalLaunch() (override, the FilesOpsSurface narrow intent): resolve-only like editorLaunch() — current area + current directory + pure gate; NotSupported surfaces the honest message as the standard OpsNotice; Ready carries areaId+kind+the exact AreaPath. No I/O, no session creation.
- FilesActions.kt: EntryActionHandlers.onTerminal; directories in the Linux area get the REAL "Open Terminal Here" action (Icons.Outlined.Terminal, verified present in material-icons-extended); Android directories keep the handler null and the sheet note is the exact honest boundary message (replaces the Phase 4 deferral "arrives in a later update" note); Linux note explains the real behavior. Regular files: nothing added (minimum scope held).
- FilesScreen.kt: onOpenTerminal param; sheet wires onTerminal ONLY when entry is a DIRECTORY AND state.areaId.kind == GUEST_LINUX; KDoc updated. Stale sheet / file entry / invalid name → honest NotSupported refusal, NEVER a fallback to the browsed location. Task ID: 22 (M7.0.0 Phase 8 — File search)
Agent: main (Super Z)
Task: Add a safe, simple, mobile-friendly file/folder NAME search to the Files explorer — recursive inside the selected storage area only, literal case-insensitive substring matching, no indexing/Room/WorkManager/content search, zero new dependencies or permissions. No approval gate between audit and implementation (new workflow).

Work Log:
- Baseline verified per instruction: HEAD 6d31eda (delivery checkpoint), accepted P7.1 code commit fb01540 (604/604 green), worklog Task 21 closed; only dev-server log noise dirty.
- AUDIT (P8-relevant surface only): StorageArea.list() returns sorted ListResult with honest errors; FsEntry reports symlinks AS NODES; SAF area has no symlink concept and maps SecurityException to honest ListResult.Error + onAccessLost; ExplorerOps.composeChild is the ONE validated child composition; explorer sort = compareBy(kind != DIRECTORY, name.lowercase(), name); FilesViewModel discipline = one serial explorerIo worker + dispatch supersession; FilesScreen = header/location/listing with zero FS access; test fakes = real temp dirs via FileDirArea.create + FakeDocumentBackend.
- ARCHITECTURE DECISION: search composes ENTIRELY from existing list() — zero new StorageArea APIs (the spec's preferred shape). One additive ExplorerCore primitive openDirectory(path: AreaPath, highlight) for result activation: AreaPath is the canonical traversal-free type (cannot represent outside the area), same safety profile as navigateUp's composed parent. State gains a trailing additive `highlight: String?` field, cleared by every ordinary navigation primitive.
- NEW files/FileSearch.kt (pure, JVM-tested, no Android imports): BFS from the area root over list(); match = entry.name.lowercase().contains(query.lowercase()) — the query is DATA (never regex/glob/shell/path; traversal targets come from listings, never from the query); SYMLINK nodes are matchable results but NEVER descended (cycles terminate by construction — no cycle bookkeeping to get wrong); SearchOutcome = Idle (blank query, ZERO scans) | Matches(query, results, truncated, scanTruncated, skippedCount, firstError) | Error(root-listing failure); limits SearchLimits(maxResults=200, maxDirectories=2000) with honest precise semantics (cap hit exactly on the last match of the last directory is NOT truncation; a dropped match always sets it); results sorted directories-first / ci-name / parent-path / exact-name; SearchResult carries the area-native parent spine (honest for SAF — no fabricated POSIX paths); FilesSearchState publication model (Idle/Running/Done/Failed).
- FilesViewModel: searchIo = its OWN serial limitedParallelism(1) worker (a long walk never delays navigation on explorerIo; search never touches core state — only read-only list() calls shared); @Volatile searchGen generation guard bumped by every search/exit/switch/SAF-change; a walk checks the gen between directories (cooperative cancel — blocking I/O never sees job cancellation) and at publication (stale results can NEVER overwrite newer state); search(raw) blank → Idle + no scan; openSearchResult = exitSearch + dispatch openDirectory(parent, highlight); switchArea/addSafFolderPicked/removeSafFolder all exitSearch() first (one area's results can never appear in another).
- FilesScreen: header search icon (becomes the close affordance in mode; area chip hidden in mode so scope stays unambiguous); SearchFieldRow = focused BasicTextField (EditorScreen's FocusRequester pattern) with "Search in <area>" placeholder + in-field clear; SearchBody renders ONLY the publication state (hint / spinner / honest limit+skip banners / no-matches / results); SearchRow = kind icon + name + relative location line; back exits search before navigation; result tap exits mode and activates. Listing/EntryRow gained the additive highlighted tint (surfaceEnv) for the activation landing.
- MainActivity: search state collected; the four callbacks wired as method references. FilesScreen signature grew additively.
- TESTS: NEW FileSearchTest (28 pins) — basic (recursive file+dir match, case-insensitivity both sides, dotfiles, honest empty, relative locations incl. root-level, deterministic ordering incl. path tie-break); safety (traversal shapes inert, shell/glob metacharacters literal BOTH directions, multi-word = one literal, structural parents-inside-area check); symlinks (node matchable never descended — duplicate-parent proof, cycle termination with self/parent/cross links, dangling safe); areas (shelf OPEN policy, guest protected prefixes searchable read-only, SAF spine honesty + no-SYMLINK-shape); errors (revoked SAF = hard Error with the area's honest sentence, failing root = Error, failed sub-dir = skippedCount+firstError with the rest kept); limits (result cap exact-count + disclosed, directory cap with pre-cap matches kept, no false truncation under defaults); cancellation (before walk → null, mid-walk WITH prior matches → null — stale never publishes, flag consulted but never fired → complete). ExplorerCoreTest +4 (openDirectory navigates+highlights, root-level works + highlight cleared by next navigation, vanished path honest with location kept, invalid highlight refused). Real bugs found during testing were TEST bugs (fixtures without a pre-created /home, a hand-miscounted ci sort order, an assertion contradicting literal-data semantics, SAF revocation message matching) — the engine needed zero changes after the cap-semantics refinement.
- GATES: focused 47/47 (FileSearchTest 28 + ExplorerCoreTest 20); FULL JVM suite FORCED --rerun-tasks: 636 tests / 0 failures / 0 errors / 0 skipped (app 491 = 459 + 32 new; terminal-emulator 145 untouched green); :app:assembleDebug BUILD SUCCESSFUL (30,309,154 B, sha256 7af912d1…01f1); built-APK manifest permission set compared programmatically (UTF-16 AXML) against the delivered P7.1 APK = IDENTICAL 6-permission set (the set has been 6 since m6.0.4; §37's "5" was a doc slip — no permission ever changed); dex scan: FileSearch/openSearchResult/guestTerminalChain all present; version stays 0.10.0-m6.0.4 / vc44 (no bump until P9).
- FREEZE AUDIT: changed files = FileSearch.kt (new), FileSearchTest.kt (new), FilesViewModel.kt, MainActivity.kt, ExplorerCore.kt, FilesScreen.kt, ExplorerCoreTest.kt, docs/TESTING.md §38 — ZERO runtime/terminal-emulator/terminal-view/keyboard/companion/packages/SAF-backend/manifest/build.gradle files; no session machinery touched; P7.1 behavior untouched (its suite green).
- docs/TESTING.md §38: 28-step device checklist (A enter/exit mode, B basic search, C result activation + highlight, D query-is-data, E per-area scope + revoked grant honesty, F limits/partials disclosures, G P2–P7.1 regression ladder).

Stage Summary:
- Phase 8 complete under the new no-approval-gate workflow: name search is recursive inside the selected area only, literal and case-insensitive, composed entirely from the unchanged Phase 2 list() abstraction (zero new storage APIs), symlink-safe by construction, honestly limited (200 matches / 2000 folders, skipped folders disclosed), and race-free via its own serial worker + generation guard. 636/636 JVM green, APK assembles with a byte-identical permission set, M6 untouched, version unchanged. Ready for P9 (integration audit + version bump 0.11.0-m7.0.0 / vc45) per plan.

Task ID: 24 (M7.0.0 Phase 8.1 — multi-select for copy/move/delete)
Agent: main (Super Z)
Task: The user's small edition on top of the P8 redo: "small edition to our file explorer option to select multiple files for copy move delete."

Work Log:
- Architecture (audit first): Phase 4 ops are one-pending-transfer clipboard + navigate + pasteHere with per-name Replace/Cancel, and delete is one-at-a-time with a confirm; the VM is the only FilesOpsSurface implementor; there is no VM-level test infra — the house pattern keeps logic in the PURE layer. So: NEW files/MultiSelectOps.kt (pure, JVM-tested, zero Android imports): buildTransfers (listing-ordered queue of fully-validated single PendingTransfers — every path re-composed through ExplorerOps.composeChild, vanished/invalid selected names become honest failures, never guessed paths), deleteAll (per-name area.delete with per-item failure aggregation — dirs take contents, symlink nodes keep targets, DENIED policy reasons carried), pastePass (the multi paste as a pure step machine: items run in queue order through the UNCHANGED ExplorerOps.checkPaste/executePaste; the FIRST collision interrupts with NeedsReplace(done, current, existingKind, target, rest); SameAsSource/vanished/invalid items fail individually without stopping the pass), summary()/bannerText()/deleteWarnings() presentation.
- FilesOpsSurface additions (additive only): selectionMode/selection/pendingCount/multiDeleteConfirm state + enterSelectionMode/toggleSelected/selectAll/exitSelectionMode/startCopySelected/startMoveSelected/openMultiDeleteConfirm/dismiss/confirm intents + OpsCommand.PasteMulti (current/target/targetDir PINNED like Paste/targetAreaId/rest/running done aggregate) + MultiDeleteConfirm(names, warnings). No existing member changed; the single-op flows are untouched code paths.
- FilesViewModel: selection lives ONLY as names of the current listing — exitSelection() hooked at every navigation/area/SAF/search boundary (openChild, navigateUp, switchArea, openSearch, openSearchResult, both SAF set-change sites) so a selection can never outlive its directory or cross an area; the multi clipboard is a private queue of validated transfers; startSelectedTransfer exits selection and stages the banner (empty/unmatched selection → honest "Nothing to …" notice); pasteHere branches on the queue → pastePass; resolveReplace(true) executes the interrupted item with replace=true then re-runs pastePass over the PINNED targetDir, chaining further collisions; resolveReplace(false) on a multi NEVER goes silent — it reports what already landed plus "Cancelled at …"; confirmMultiDelete runs deleteAll inside the standard runOp (loading stage + refresh-after-operation).
- FilesScreen/FilesActions: header select toggle (hidden in search mode and for empty locations); selection bar replaces the location row (count + All/Copy/Move/Delete, disabled at zero); rows toggle in mode (checkbox icons, accent tint, no nav, no sheet affordance); PendingBanner gained the count (single wording byte-identical via MultiSelectOps.bannerText); ConfirmMultiDeleteDialog with the honest count, first three names + "and N more", and the deduped per-kind warnings; Back leaves selection before navigation.
- TESTS: NEW MultiSelectOpsTest (17 pins over real temp dirs) — queue building (listing order + kinds, vanished/path-like selected names disclosed, empty ok), deleteAll (files+nested tree, symlink nodes only with self/cross links, vanished+DENIED per-item failures without aborting), pastePass (copy keeps sources in engine order, move clears them, first-collision interruption with exact done/rest and nothing after it executed, the VM resolve composition replace+rest aggregate, SameAsSource per-item failures, vanished sources per-item, invalid ".." item, cross-area folder move), summary/banner/warnings exact texts. Bugs found during testing were TEST bugs (a SameAsSource fixture that should expect BOTH items to fail; a policy-denial fixture that never composed the protected path; one backwards assertNotNull) — the engine needed zero changes after they were fixed.
- GATES: focused files-package suite 95/95 (MultiSelectOps 17 + ExplorerOps 31 + ExplorerCore 19 + FileSearch 28); FULL JVM suite FORCED --rerun-tasks: 653/653 green (app 508 = 491 + 17; terminal-emulator 145 untouched); :app:assembleDebug green — 30,451,017 B, sha256 f316ec67…bfaf2; versionCode 44 / 0.10.0-m6.0.4 unchanged; manifest permission set = the unchanged 6 (programmatic badging check); embedded rev=2 layer asset sha-exact 898131ff…/17,920,000 B; dex contains MultiSelectOps/startCopySelected/confirmMultiDelete alongside the P8 (openSearchResult/FileSearch) and P7.1 (guestTerminalChain/newSessionMatchingCurrent) symbols.
- FREEZE AUDIT: changed files = MultiSelectOps.kt (new), MultiSelectOpsTest.kt (new), FilesOpsSurface.kt, FilesViewModel.kt, FilesScreen.kt, FilesActions.kt, docs/TESTING.md §39, worklog — ZERO runtime/terminal-emulator/terminal-view/keyboard/companion/packages/SAF-backend/manifest/build.gradle files; no session machinery; P8 search and P7.1 terminal behavior re-proven green by the full suite. docs/TESTING.md §39 = 25-step device checklist (A enter/exit, B multi delete, C multi copy incl. cross-area, D multi move, E collisions incl. honest partial-cancel, F scope & safety, G regression ladder).

Stage Summary:
- Phase 8.1 complete: multi-select is the existing per-entry engine executed N times with an honest aggregate — selection is names-only and dies at every boundary, collisions reuse the one Replace dialog, partial outcomes are always disclosed, zero new storage APIs/permissions/dependencies, M6 and P7.x untouched.

- PART A audit on the restored history: HEAD 0882dd1 is a platform snapshot commit over b1a6a6e (delivery/protocol noise only — page.tsx/scripts/logs; ZERO source diffs vs the e7f2630 delivery tip); working tree clean; worklog through Task 25; commit chain fb01540 → 51cd18b → e7f2630 → 48ac398 → b1a6a6e intact.
- SEARCH SCROLL ROOT CAUSE found: the app mounts ONE keyboard deck at the composition root over EVERY screen (m4.0.12, system IME hard-blocked), and every screen is required to push itself above it via keyboardBottomInset — TerminalScreen, EditorScreen and CompanionLayer all do; FilesScreen NEVER received the inset. The Files viewport (listing AND search results) therefore extended behind the deck (and behind the gesture bar when the deck is collapsed): a result list shorter than the too-tall viewport had nothing to scroll while its tail sat hidden under the deck (the exact "results appear but cannot scroll" symptom), and a long one could never reveal its last rows. Fix: FilesScreen takes keyboardBottomInset (MainActivity passes the measured deck height) and applies the established Terminal/Editor rule on its root Column (deck inset when mounted, navigationBarsPadding when 0) — the fix repairs the explorer listing's tail reachability too (a genuine P9 integration defect, not search-only).
- LONG-PRESS: SearchRow gained combinedClickable (tap = the unchanged P8 activation; long-press = actions). The long-press lands on the result's parent through the EXISTING openSearchResult path (exit search + core.openDirectory(parent, highlight)) and parks the entry name in a UI-local searchActionTarget; when the FRESH listing of that parent arrives, the real FsEntry is promoted into the SAME `selected` slot that drives the explorer's EntryActionSheet — so Open / Open Terminal Here (Linux-area directories only) / New folder / New file / Copy / Move / Share / Export / Rename / Delete all route through the EXISTING per-entry ops with the landed directory as context (names resolve against the browsed path; a vanished entry never opens a sheet over a stale snapshot). Zero new operations logic, zero new storage APIs.
- REDUNDANT CLOSE removed: SearchFieldRow's KeyboardArrowLeft "Close search" (the duplicate of the header X) deleted; the header X remains the ONE close affordance (system Back still exits search); the in-field ✕ is documented as the query-only clear. Search triggering untouched: same header icon, no Search button, no submit UI (PART C honored).
- Integration audit (PART D) beyond the scroll fix: no further release blockers — the P8 search machinery (serial worker, generation guard, honest limits/errors), P8.1 selection boundaries, P7.1 terminal resolution, and SAF labels all re-verified by the full suite; known non-blockers left alone deliberately: a search walk keeps running (bounded, read-only, generation-guarded) if the user leaves Files via the header back; dialog windows render above the deck (pre-existing P4 behavior, device-accepted since P4).
- Version bump (PART E): build.gradle.kts versionCode 44→45, versionName 0.10.0-m6.0.4→0.11.0-m7.0.0 (the ONLY legitimate code-side location); release metadata synced: runtime-tests/device_gate.sh + scripts/rehearse_device_gate.sh app-stamp expectations now accept 0.11.0-m7.0.0 (vc45) while still accepting vc44; historical docs/scripts/CHANGELOG entries untouched; docs/CHANGELOG.md gains the 0.11.0-m7.0.0 release entry; docs/TESTING.md gains §40 (27-step P9/M7.0 final device checklist: A scrolling, B controls, C long-press actions incl. symlink/special-char/protected/vanished, D multi-select ladder, E editor, F terminal, G Android/SAF + version/permission stamp).
- Toolchain rebuilt after the reset (scripts/install_toolchain.sh, foreground staged runs: Temurin jdk-21.0.12.1+1 + cmdline-tools 11076708 + platform-36 + build-tools 36.0.0; AGP auto-provisioned NDK 28.2.13676358 + build-tools 35 during compile; local.properties written).
- GATES (PART F): FULL JVM suite FORCED --rerun-tasks: 653 tests / 0 failures / 0 errors / 0 skipped effective (the one assumption-skip, GuestGlibcRuntimeTest's built-APK asset pin, re-ran GREEN after assembleDebug — it verifies the embedded rev=2 layer asset by sha inside the built APK); :app:assembleDebug BUILD SUCCESSFUL — app-debug.apk 30,164,873 B, sha256 8826d30dfc23dc6318e8306e5e17ea16a8500ea671217b6d24367afd4f53dd08; aapt2 badging: versionCode='45' versionName='0.11.0-m7.0.0' targetSdk 28; permission set = the UNCHANGED 6 (ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE, FOREGROUND_SERVICE, FOREGROUND_SERVICE_SPECIAL_USE, INTERNET, POST_NOTIFICATIONS, DYNAMIC_RECEIVER_NOT_EXPORTED_PERMISSION) — no MANAGE/broad-legacy storage permissions; dex symbol scan: P9 (searchActionTarget, keyboardBottomInset) + P8 (FileSearch, openSearchResult, openDirectory) + P8.1 (MultiSelectOps, confirmMultiDelete) + P7/P7.1 (TerminalLaunchSupport, guestTerminalChain, newSessionMatchingCurrent) + P6 (EditorLaunch) ALL PRESENT; M6 FREEZE AUDIT CLEAN — changed paths: FilesScreen.kt, MainActivity.kt, build.gradle.kts, device_gate.sh, rehearse_device_gate.sh, docs/TESTING.md, docs/CHANGELOG.md, worklog, audit/delivery scripts — ZERO runtime/terminal-emulator/terminal-view/keyboard/companion/packages/SAF-backend/manifest files.

Work Log:
- AUDIT: HEAD 63c9c28 / clean tree / P1 chain 3abb2e8 → ebe8c77 → 63c9c28 (679 tests, vc45). Screenshot read directly: the deleted-seed restore row ("GitHub" + "Not on this install") collapsed one character per line with a stretched full-width Restore button.
- ROOT CAUSE (exact): MidnightQuietButton (MidnightPage.kt) hard-fills its width from the inside — Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth()); it is a PAGE-level component. P1's deleted-seed row placed it in an unweighted Row slot, it consumed the entire row, and the Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) text column measured at zero width → per-character word breaking. Confirmed against the working rows in the same screenshot (Z.ai row renders correctly — same primitives minus the button).
- FIX (structural): NEW shared LauncherSettingRow = the brief's slot contract ONCE (fixed 36dp icon → weight(1f) text column → optional intrinsically-sized action slot → optional fixed control slot); CompanionRow + the deleted-seed restore row + all built-in tool rows render through it; Restore = compact RowTextAction (the row's existing action pattern); subtitles no longer clip (natural wrap); the deleted-seed badge shares ONE collision space with existing definitions; dimmed tone for not-on-this-install. Zero arbitrary widths — the expanding-component prohibition IS the fix.
- ANTIGRAVITY: grounded in docs/ANTIGRAVITY-PLATFORM.md §1 (Google's own installer names the binary `agy`) — registry entry id='agy' (the id==probeName invariant holds), displayName 'Antigravity'; Gemini CLI removed from ids/commands/display names; CompanionTabs' 'Gemini' quick-add TEMPLATE untouched (frozen M6 companion package, a different system); stale persisted 'gemini' hide ids inert by construction.
- ICONS: 14 curated marks (4 companions + 10 registry tools) built by NEW scripts/make_launcher_icons.py from OFFICIAL first-party origins (URLs documented; SVGs rasterized via cairosvg at build time; two documented monochrome treatments: GitHub's dark-scheme white mark on the GitHub-dark tile, Codex white-on-dark from the official OpenAI flower source after the 48px codex favicon proved too small/invisible), normalized to 192×192 lossless WebP (~125 KB total, one uniform content box so wide marks cannot dominate). NEW launchers/LauncherBundledIcons (static curated-id→asset map, custom ids never hit it) + LauncherTileIcon gains the bundled layer in the ONE chain: imported copy → bundled mark → badge. HomeScreen tiles carry launcherId. Zero runtime network; trademark note + provenance in docs/THIRD_PARTY.md. Two icon-audit rounds caught and fixed: invisible dark marks (GitHub/Codex), the wrong Anthropic mark for builtin-claude, a recolor-alpha bug (RGB source).
- TESTS +11 → FULL JVM FORCED --rerun-tasks 690 effective (app 545 + te 145) / 0 failures / 0 errors / 0 skipped: CommandAppsTest (antigravity present-and-shaped, gemini absent with no stale trace, probe-gated), LauncherModelsTest (stale retired-default hide ids inert; restore resurrects CURRENT ids only), NEW LauncherBundledIconsTest 6 (map covers EXACTLY the curated set both directions, safe paths, traversal/junk/'gemini' never resolve, source-tree assets exist + directory carries exactly the mapped set, built APK packages all 14 — the vc40/vc41 lesson at icon weight), NEW LauncherRowLayoutTest 2 (the honest structural pin: this JVM suite has no Robolectric/device so Compose UI tests are not practical — pins weight(1f) on the shared row's text column + zero page-level expanding buttons inside any Row scope, comments/strings stripped).

Agent: main (Super Z)

Standing no-gate workflow, deliver end-to-end.

Work Log:
- AUDIT (PART A): tip 8354df6 over the P2.2 chain (6004805 → c57c8ee → c6dea32), working tree clean (dev.log only); delivery surface healthy. Keyboard architecture mapped end-to-end: the ONE visibility owner is `keyboardExpanded` in PocketShellRoot (rememberSaveable); deck mounts `if (keyboardExpanded)`; inset = deck onSizeChanged → keyboardInsetPx → keyboardInset Dp → keyboardBottomInset to Terminal/Files/Editor/CompanionLayer (all 5 consumers read; M7.0 Files fix lives in FilesScreen — untouched); manual controls = deck toggle key + floating [⌨] icon + terminal-canvas tap + KeyboardInputRouter.onWebFocusGained. Settings = SettingsRepository (DataStore `settings`, string prefs) + SettingsViewModel stateIn + MidnightSwitch rows. Notification infra = TerminalService foreground channel + Toasts + the Home LaunchErrorBanner — no permission-free global notifier → in-app root banner chosen (zero permissions). NO existing external-keyboard detection. Baseline focused keyboard suites green.
- DESIGN (PART B, locked before code): ExternalKeyboardDetector = pure coroutine core over an injected queryConnected scan (JVM-testable) — every InputDevice event (re)starts a 400 ms stability window; only a scan still unchanged after the quiet window flips the state, at most ONE transition; duplicates coalesce; sub-window BT flaps never flicker; launch scan = the first evaluation (start()); no polling. Predicate: SOURCE_KEYBOARD bit + KEYBOARD_TYPE_ALPHABETIC + !isVirtual — rejects touchscreens/mice/gamepads/joysticks/virtual devices/button clusters (gpio-keys), accepts USB/BT/dock/DeX composites. ExternalKeyboardViewModel = the Android glue (InputManager.InputDeviceListener registered in init on the main handler, unregistered in onCleared, rescan() for ON_RESUME; root-scoped ViewModel → rotation never re-fires notices). Visibility: the root stays the ONE owner — ExternalKeyboardPolicy (SUPPRESS/RESTORE/NONE) resolves (connected, autoEnabled, suppressionActive); preExternalExpanded (rememberSaveable) = the suppression memory recording the user's pre-connect manual state; an explicit user reopen during suppression CANCELS it (openKeyboardManually funnel for ALL user writes: terminal onKeyboardExpandedChange, deck onToggleExpanded, floating icon); web-focus auto-open is GATED while suppressed (hardware keyboard types into WebView inputs; no deck pop-up). Notice policy: every stable false→true transition emits ONE ExternalKeyboardNotice (seq); disconnects restore silently; UI auto-dismisses in 4.5 s.
- IMPLEMENTATION (commit 93ee631): NEW keyboard/ExternalKeyboard.kt (predicate + scanner + detector + policy), NEW ExternalKeyboardViewModel.kt (listener glue), NEW ui/system/ExternalKeyboardNoticeBar.kt (in-app banner: "External keyboard detected — the on-screen keyboard has been turned off. You can change this in Settings.", auto-dismiss 4.5 s, Settings action navigates), SettingsRepository +auto_hide_keyboard_on_external (default ON: absent ≠ "false") + setter, SettingsViewModel +flow/setter, SettingsScreen +Keyboard section (On-screen keyboard toggle, MidnightSwitch), MainActivity root wiring (policy LaunchedEffect on (connected, auto), preExternalExpanded saveable, ON_RESUME rescan observer, openKeyboardManually funnel, gated web-focus hook, notice banner + auto-dismiss at root).
- TESTS (PART E): +4 suites / 38 tests — ExternalKeyboardPredicateTest (11: the full false-positive table incl. keyboard+mouse composite → true, gpio/virtual/none → false); ExternalKeyboardDetectorTest (10: virtual-time debounce via kotlinx-coroutines-test — connect after the window, duplicate bursts coalesce to ONE transition/one notice, sub-window BT flap = no transition, beyond-window flap = both, disconnect silent + reconnect one NEW notice, events before start ignored, stop cancels, dismiss clears notice without touching state); ExternalKeyboardPolicyTest (7: decision table + full-cycle scenarios incl. manual-hidden restore (not blind open), auto-OFF never touches the deck, override-wins-on-disconnect, reconnect re-suppresses, mid-session toggle immediate); ExternalKeyboardIntegrationTest (10: structural contract over the real sources — policy effect keyed on (connected, auto), openKeyboardManually funnel on all three user paths, gated web-focus, ON_RESUME rescan, notice mounted with dismiss + Settings nav, listener register/unregister + launch scan, event-driven no-polling, DataStore key + default-ON, wired MidnightSwitch toggle, no competing keyboard-state system). Read the whole P3 stack (ExternalKeyboard.kt detector + policy, ExternalKeyboardViewModel, MainActivity root wiring, TerminalScreen tap path, SettingsRepository/ViewModel/Screen, NoticeBar, manifest). Static review found the listener/scan/policy logic theoretically sound — the failure had to be empirical. Findings, each verified in code:
  1. PRIMARY DEFECT: TerminalScreen.kt:216 — every terminal-canvas tap called onKeyboardExpandedChange(true) → openKeyboardManually → preExternalExpanded=null — the tap CANCELLED the suppression. On a terminal app the canvas tap is the most-used gesture: the deck hid on connect and popped straight back up on the next touch. The auto-hide was invisible in the app's primary workflow.
  2. DEBOUNCE STARVATION: the 400 ms stability window restarted on EVERY input-device event with no ceiling — periodic onInputDeviceChanged re-announcements (Bluetooth LE HID reality on OEM stacks) could defer the confirm forever; detection never fires.
  3. SINGLE MECHANISM: only InputManager.InputDeviceListener drove foregrounded re-evaluation; OEM stacks can miss listener callbacks for BT HID (re)connection. Manifest configChanges already declares keyboard|keyboardHidden|navigation (no activity recreation — verified), but the configuration-change path was UNUSED.
  4. SPEC GAP: no disconnect notice (spec requires "External keyboard disconnected.").
  5. SPEC GAP: no persistent On-screen keyboard preference — runtime-only deck state + an auto-hide opt-out; the disconnect restore could force the deck on against the user's wishes.
  Research (web): confirmed Configuration.keyboard is unreliable on some OEMs (stays KEYBOARD_NOKEYS) and onConfigurationChanged gaps are known — hence the fix treats config-change as a SECOND trigger into the same coalescing detector, not as a truth source; the strict predicate (SOURCE_KEYBOARD + KEYBOARD_TYPE_ALPHABETIC + !isVirtual) is correct for real full keyboards (GENERIC.kcm fallback) and was retained.
- IMPLEMENT (one authoritative state system): NEW ExternalKeyboardVisibilityModel in keyboard/ExternalKeyboard.kt — externalKeyboardConnected + onscreenKeyboardUserEnabled + manualRequest → shouldShowOnscreenKeyboard = manualRequest ?: (userEnabled && !connected); hardware transitions and preference changes clear the manual request; canvasTapReopen() GATED inside the model (connected → refuses; else reopens like m4.0.12). The M7.1 ExternalKeyboardPolicy (SUPPRESS/RESTORE/NONE) and the root's preExternalExpanded/local keyboardExpanded state RETIRED. Detector reworked: directional ExternalKeyboardNotice(seq, direction) — BOTH directions emit once per confirmed transition; NEW confirm-deadline mechanism (DEFAULT_CONFIRM_DEADLINE_MS=2000 anchored at the first unconfirmed event of a burst; window and deadline race, one confirm()) — event storms can no longer starve detection while sub-window flaps still never flicker. ViewModel reworked: owns detector + visibility model; registers InputDeviceListener (all three callbacks) AND the NEW Application-level ComponentCallbacks2.onConfigurationChanged cross-check (unregistered in onCleared); bridges detector.connected + DataStore.onscreenKeyboardEnabled into the model; exposes exactly externalKeyboardConnected / onscreenKeyboardUserEnabled / shouldShowOnscreenKeyboard + requestShow/canvasTapReopen/rescan/dismissNotice. MainActivity rewired: keyboardExpanded is now the local ALIAS of the model's shouldShow (no local copy); openKeyboardManually = requestShow (explicit, ungated); the terminal canvas rides onCanvasTapReopen → canvasTapReopen (GATED); web-focus gated on !externalKeyboardConnected; notice bar renders BOTH directions with preference-aware copy. Settings: new DataStore key onscreen_keyboard_enabled (default ON; the auto_hide key retired from code), toggle retitled with the preference semantics (SettingsScreen param renames). NoticeBar: direction + keyboardAvailable params with the spec's texts.
- TESTS: ExternalKeyboardVisibilityModelTest NEW (14: the full decision matrix incl. all four spec transitions, request lifecycle, same-value no-ops, preference immutability, the canvas-tap gate both ways). ExternalKeyboardDetectorTest rewritten (14: directional notices, disconnect-emits-DISCONNECTED, the storm/deadline proofs — endless 300 ms storm lands the transition exactly once, the deadline fires at the ceiling, a single event still confirms through the window, disconnect-under-storm lands, the M7.1 semantics that survive). ExternalKeyboardIntegrationTest rewritten (11: root observes the model's shouldShow with NO local state/policy, requestShow+canvasTapReopen funnel pins, gated web-focus, ON_RESUME rescan, both-direction notice wiring, listener + config-callback register/unregister, no-polling + deadline + predicate pins, onscreen_keyboard_enabled DataStore pins, detector-stays-visibility-free). ExternalKeyboardPredicateTest unchanged (11). ExternalKeyboardPolicyTest DELETED with the policy. Two test-side fix rounds: a non-compiling leftover line and a mis-pinned predicate substring; one OOM-killed run left stale XMLs that briefly mis-reported failures — resolved by the daemon-less rerun recipe.
- REGRESSION: FULL JVM suite forced --rerun-tasks — app 601/601 + terminal-emulator 145/145 = 746/746 green, 0 failures, 0 errors, at the fix tip.
- VERSION (sub-milestone precedent): versionCode 47 / versionName 0.11.2-m7.1.1 in app/build.gradle.kts; runtime-tests/device_gate.sh app-stamp gate accepts 0.11.2-m7.1.1 (versionCode 47) with older gates retained. Fix commit 3cbfec2.
- APK AUDITS (on the exact fix bytes): :app:assembleDebug green — app-debug.apk 30,451,377 B sha256 5d876141…e5dc1; aapt2 badging versionCode='47' versionName='0.11.2-m7.1.1' targetSdk 28; the UNCHANGED 6-permission set; exactly 26 launcher_icons asset entries (13 dark + 13 light); dex carries ExternalKeyboardViewModel/Detector/Notice/Scanner/VisibilityModel; apksigner cert d96a6f66…8bf659 unchanged (in-place update chain intact).
- DOCS: TESTING §47 = the M7.1.1 section (§47.1 the five audit findings with file-level evidence; §47.2 the state model + the 746/746 record; §47.3 the 16-step MANDATORY real-device gate: USB flow incl. the canvas-tap-stays-hidden step, Bluetooth flow, launch-attached, the disconnect direction incl. backgrounded reconnect, the 4-step Settings matrix, process-death preference check, and the regression sweep); CHANGELOG [0.11.2-m7.1.1]; ROADMAP M7.1.1 section (M7.2 stays NOT started); README status paragraph refreshed.

Task ID: 38 (M7.2 P0 — notification & agent-activity architecture audit)
Agent: main (Super Z)
Task: The user's M7.2 P0 mandate: audit + design only (no implementation) — trace the real session architecture, find what PocketShell can actually know, audit the launcher→agent path, classify agent-detection feasibility, assess waiting-for-input honestly, audit background/notification infrastructure, propose the authoritative state model and M7.2 architecture, validate the phase plan, deliver a NewGitBundle. Stop before P1.

Work Log:
- BASELINE: HEAD d4cf363 (M7.1.1 fix tip + worklog record), tree clean, versionCode 47 / 0.11.2-m7.1.1, tags M7.1-release + M7.1.1-fix. No frozen boundary touched; zero production code changed in P0.
- PART A (session architecture, all verified in code): two process domains — PTY sessions (TerminalSessionManager object owner → TerminalSession → JNI fork/execvp on /dev/ptmx, three I/O threads incl. the TermSessionWaiter whose JNI.waitFor/waitpid delivers MSG_PROCESS_EXITED with a real exit code, WEXITSTATUS or negated WTERMSIG) and package ops (ProcessBuilderGuestCommandRunner, non-PTY). Key structural fact: for guest sessions the direct child is PROOT (guest command is a descendant); the PTY child forks lazily at first updateSize, which is why launch commands ride argv (sh -l -c "<chain>"), never PTY writes. No persistence anywhere; START_NOT_STICKY; sessions die with the process.
- PART B (capability line): SESSION_EXISTS / SESSION_RUNNING (direct child) / SESSION_STOPPED (finish vs close) = RELIABLE now; exit code real but not surfaced into SessionEntry (isFinished is Boolean); COMMAND_RUNNING / SHELL_IDLE / agent-level COMPLETED/FAILED = NOT knowable today (waitpid sees one pid; no child tracking); rejected as unprovable: output-silence heuristics.
- PART C (launcher→session): full path traced (HomeScreen tiles → openCommandApp/openCustomTool/openCatalogApp → launchGuestCommand probe+prep+spawn); the ONLY surviving identity is SessionEntry.label (= displayName); guestSessionIds is ViewModel-scoped and lost on recreation (GUEST_SESSION_LABEL string fallback); structured launcher ids never cross into the session layer; TerminalSession.mSessionName never set; cleanest attach point = TerminalSessionManager.spawn (~3 call sites). Real registry audited: 9 entries (hermes, opencode, claude, zcode, kilo, cline, agy, codex, qwen).
- PART D/E (manual vs launcher, feasibility): manual command detection by keystroke parsing REJECTED (heuristic); real-signal Tier 2 = /proc descendant scanning (real host procfs is always bound into interactive guests; same-UID visibility precedent = TerminalSession.getCwd) + shell-integration marks in launch chains; Tier 1 = session-level lifecycle + spawn metadata (enough for "launched as X is running/ended"); per-agent matrix written with the binary-shape device checks honestly marked unverified.
- PART F (waiting-for-input): NO real signal exists; bell (onBell) is real but currently Log.d-discarded and semantically unbounded; process state char cannot discriminate; verdict = hints at most, never proof; P7 must be conditional or descoped.
- PART G/H (background + notifications): TerminalService specialUse FGS (channel terminal_sessions, IMPORTANCE_LOW, id 1, no-extras PendingIntent) started iff ≥1 session; keeps backgrounded sessions alive; no WakeLocks/Alarms/WorkManager anywhere; process death loses everything honestly. POST_NOTIFICATIONS declared but NEVER requested at runtime (no permission launcher exists) — the single largest P1 gap. No receivers; MainActivity singleTask ignores intent extras (onNewIntent unhandled) — tap routing needs new plumbing.
- PART I/J (proposed model, design only): extend TerminalSessionManager (the ONE owner — no second source of truth, the M7.1.1 lesson) with SpawnOrigin/AgentHint/exitStatus on SessionEntry; derived projections only (AgentActivityRepository); NotificationCoordinator edge-triggered off state transitions with NotificationPreferences DataStore gates; ProcessTreeScanner as the graded-evidence Tier 2 component. Provable transitions pinned: SPAWNING→RUNNING→FINISHED(code)→REMOVED.
- PART K/L (risks + phase validation): 14 risks recorded (multi-agent-sequential-in-one-session = the P6 design problem: agent end ≠ session end under the exec chain); phase plan validated with 4 adjustments — P1 gains the permission request, P2 stays session-level + exit-code surfacing, P3 splits P3a (metadata) / P3b (device-verified scanner), P7 conditional. No reordering needed otherwise.
- ARTIFACT: docs/M7.2-P0-AUDIT.md (14 sections, every claim cited to a file/symbol) + docs/ROADMAP.md M7.2 section updated (P0 complete, P1 next, code NOT started). No tests added (PART M: no speculative tests for unbuilt architecture). Committed as c8d0059; tree clean.
- DELIVERY: pocketshell-m7.2-p0.gitbundle cut from c8d0059 (complete history); staged in public/ (direct download URL) + download/ (master) + upload/ (insurance); clone drill green (fresh clone lands on c8d0059 with the audit doc + vc47 stamp in the cloned tree). Server serving the M7.1.1 page set unchanged (no page re-pin — P0 has no user-facing UI; the bundle is a direct-URL artifact per PART P).

- BASELINE: HEAD 1d0741c (the P1 record tip), tree clean except the running delivery server's dev.log; P1 payload tip a7c635e confirmed in the delivery records; versionCode 47 / 0.11.2-m7.1.1. Authoritative P2 spec re-read from the repo: ROADMAP M7.2 section ("P2 — session lifecycle engine & structured exit status: SpawnOrigin/AgentHint metadata through the real spawn path; waitpid exit status surfaced as a structured value; explicit race-safe transitions; lifecycle observation") + audit §1.3/§5/§10.3/PART I-J-L. Source recon verified (not trusted): TerminalSessionManager (object owner, isFinished Boolean), TerminalSession.java (lazy fork at initializeEmulator; TermSessionWaiter → JNI.waitFor → MSG_PROCESS_EXITED; getExitStatus valid only when !isRunning), termux.c waitFor (WEXITSTATUS / negated WTERMSIG), PocketShellSessionClient (setTerminalShellPid only Log.d — the discarded fork signal), TerminalViewModel (5 spawn paths), TerminalService (FGS sync on every entry, channel/id untouched), all isFinished consumers (all survive via a derived getter).
- IMPLEMENT (3 commits): 0c9a792 implementation + 577e1e9 tests + 133e656 docs/tooling.
  (1) terminal/SessionLifecycle.kt — the P2 vocabulary: SessionPhase (STARTING/RUNNING/FINISHED; REMOVED = list absence + event, per §10.3), ExitStatus (Exited(code)/Signaled(signal) + fromWaitpidRaw — exactly the JNI.waitFor contract, no invented "unknown" variant), SessionLifecycleState (private constructor → invalid combinations unrepresentable: FINISHED always carries a status, STARTING/RUNNING never do) with the PURE transition machine (onProcessStarted/onProcessFinished → LifecycleTransition.Accepted/Rejected-with-reason), SpawnOrigin (Shell/LinuxShell/FilesTerminal/CommandApp(id)/CatalogApp(id)/CustomTool(id) — the six real launch paths, sealed), AgentHint(displayName/command/matchedBy) + AgentMatchedBy (ONLY LAUNCH_METADATA declared in P2; the procfs grades are P3b compile-time extensions, deliberately not pre-invented), SessionLifecycleEvent (Started/Finished/Removed, each carrying the full identity block so consumers never re-look-up by id).
